- **bauplan**: Bauplan draws how an AI agent's code works, as a block-diagram overview plus a detailed function-level view showing where every LLM call, tool call, external API call and database read or write happens, what goes in and comes out, the conditions, loops and model-made decisions, each record's status lifecycle, and the usage numbers of every model call, all checked against the code so it never quietly goes stale. Works for any language or framework. Use it whenever someone wants to see, explain, document, review or onboard onto how their agent, pipeline or LLM workflow works ("draw the workflow… ([Sailaukan/bauplan](https://github.com/Sailaukan/bauplan/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **bauplan**: Plant eine größere Aufgabe vor jeder Änderung - Ziel, überprüfbare Pakete, Nicht-Ziele, Abhängigkeiten, Risiken und Prüfbefehle - und wartet auf Freigabe. Verwenden bei "Bauplan", "plan das erst", "größere Aufgabe", "neues Feature", "Umbau", "Projekt starten", "bevor du baust". ([TIM-Intelegence-market/tim-ai-platform](https://github.com/TIM-Intelegence-market/tim-ai-platform/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/bauplan))
- **bauplan-dag-optimization**: Optimizes an existing, poorly structured Bauplan DAG so it runs faster without changing its outputs. Reads the code and profiles the data, then restructures the models, decides what is materialized, narrows inputs with projections and filters, merges or splits projects, and proposes a data-driven partitioning plan with concrete columns and transforms. Use whenever the user has a working pipeline and wants it faster or leaner on Bauplan, for example 'optimize this DAG', 'this pipeline is slow, speed it up', 'we write too many intermediate tables', 'should I partition this table', 'make this pi… ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-dag-optimization) · ★18)
- **bauplan-data-assessment**: Assesses whether a business question can be answered with data available in a Bauplan lakehouse. Maps business concepts to tables and columns, checks data quality on the relevant subset, validates semantic fit, and renders a verdict: answerable, partially answerable, or not answerable. Produces a structured feasibility report. Use when a user brings a business question, asks 'can we answer this', wants to know if the data supports an analysis, or before building a one-off analysis or pipeline. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-data-assessment) · ★18)
- **bauplan-data-pipeline**: Creates bauplan data pipeline projects with SQL and Python models. Use when starting a new pipeline, defining DAG transformations, writing models, or setting up bauplan project structure from scratch. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-data-pipeline) · ★18)
- **bauplan-data-quality-checks**: Generates data quality check code for bauplan pipelines and ingestion workflows. Invoked by the bauplan-data-pipeline and bauplan-safe-ingestion skills, or directly by the user. Produces expectations.py for pipelines or validation logic for WAP scripts. Output is always code, never reports. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-data-quality-checks) · ★18)
- **bauplan-data-semantics**: Reads and drafts the semantic layer of a Bauplan lakehouse — the table-level and column-level documentation carried in Iceberg as `comment` and field `doc`. Use to find out what a column means before querying it, to diagnose a query that returned a plausible but wrong number, or to draft documentation for columns whose meaning is not obvious from name and type. Also invoked by bauplan-data-pipeline and bauplan-migrate-to-typed-sdk when a pipeline needs documentation written. Read-only: drafts documentation text, never edits model code or runs pipelines. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-data-semantics) · ★18)
- **bauplan-debug-and-fix-pipeline**: Diagnose a failed Bauplan job, pin the exact data state, collect evidence, apply a minimal fix, and rerun. Evidence first, changes second. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-debug-and-fix-pipeline) · ★18)
- **bauplan-explore-data**: Explores data in a Bauplan lakehouse safely using the Bauplan Python SDK. Use to inspect namespaces, tables, schemas, samples, and profiling queries; and to export larger result sets to files. Read-only exploration only; no writes or pipeline runs. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-explore-data) · ★18)
- **bauplan-migrate-to-typed-sdk**: Migrates an existing bauplan pipeline project from the old SDK style (0.1.x or 0.2.x) to the typed SDK (0.3.+). Use when asked to migrate a pipeline to the new SDK, add type annotations to a bauplan project, upgrade to bauplan 0.3, or adopt TableSchema / the semantic layer. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-migrate-to-typed-sdk) · ★18)
- **bauplan-safe-ingestion**: Ingest data from object storage (S3 or GCS) into Bauplan safely using branch isolation and quality checks before publishing. Use when loading new data from S3 or GCS (s3:// or gs:// paths), importing parquet/csv/jsonl files, or when the user needs to safely load data with validation before merging to main. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-safe-ingestion) · ★18)

Nutzt vorhandene Falldaten, entscheidet die maßgeblichen rechtlichen Abzweigungen und verbindet passende Fachprüfungen mit einem vollständig ausgearbeiteten Ergebnis einschließlich Belegen, Gegenposition und Fristen. - **bauschritt**: Verwenden für einen Bau-Auftrag oder ein Vorhaben aus ROADMAP.md (JETZT) — Trigger «Baue …», «bau das», «nimm das nächste Vorhaben», «Auftrag aufnehmen», «neuer Auftrag», «neuer Wunsch», «Fahrplan anlegen», «Dispatch», «Sub-Agent beauftragen», «räum die Roadmap auf». Lebenszyklus Einstieg → Bau → Prüfung → Landung → Weiterbau → Abschluss; dazu Aufnahme und Einordnung (§14.1–14.3), Definition of Done (§14.4), Trailer (§14.5), Delegation und Kontext-Hygiene (§14.6), Rahmen vor Feature (§10). - **bayesian-stats**: Use this Skill when the user needs to build Bayesian statistical models: prior selection, posterior sampling with MCMC (NUTS), convergence diagnostics, posterior predictive checks, LOO-CV model comparison, hierarchical models, and Bayesian A/B testing. Covers PyMC 5.x and ArviZ. ([xjtulyc/awesome-rosetta-skills](https://github.com/xjtulyc/awesome-rosetta-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/03-mathematics/bayesian-stats) · ★37)

- **bayesian-thinking**: 用贝叶斯思维看待判断:你的结论不是非黑即白的一次定论,而是随证据不断更新的概率。当用户说「我就是觉得肯定是这样」「这事到底行不行」「我已经认定了」「看到一个反例就全盘推翻」,或在用「非对即错」的方式判断一件其实充满不确定的事时使用。即使没明说,只要用户把一个概率性判断当成了非黑即白的定论,就应主动用上它。 ([Beckettsapere824/cocrea-skills](https://github.com/Beckettsapere824/cocrea-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lens/bayesian-thinking))
- **bayesian-tpe-hyperparameter-search**: Use when training retention-time (or other regression) models whose hyperparameters (DNN layer sizes, dropout, learning schedules, GBM depths) must be tuned to maximize median absolute error rather than MAE, before nested-CV evaluation. - **bayesian-update**: Revises a stated probability in light of one new piece of evidence using the odds form of Bayes' rule — prior odds × Bayes factor = posterior odds — and reports the posterior in both relative and absolute terms with a sensitivity sweep over the prior and the likelihood ratio. Use when a belief has to move by a defensible amount: "update my prior", "how much should this evidence change my estimate?", "what's the posterior probability?", "run a Bayesian update on this". Not for enumerating and ranking several rival explanations (use `analysis-of-competing-hypotheses`) or for scoring forecasts a… ([radarist/structured-analytic-skills](https://github.com/radarist/structured-analytic-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bayesian-update) · ★8)
- **bayesian-updating**: Runs belief revision as a decision discipline: starts a question from an explicit prior (base-rate anchor from reference-class-forecasting), weighs each piece of evidence by how surprising it would be under each hypothesis, updates with count tables or the odds shortcut, not formulas, grades evidence in Bayes-factor bands (barely-worth-mentioning to very strong), and keeps a Tetlock-style update journal: small, frequent, logged revisions scored at resolution. - **bazi-qiongtong-jin**: 用于用户拿庚金或辛金日主、问某个月（正月…十二月、或三春／三夏／三秋／三冬）该取哪几个字、次序怎么排时。触发信号包括「庚金日主正月取什么」「辛金八月专用壬水怎么讲」「秋金锐锐最为奇什么意思」「庚金五月专用壬水癸次之」「辛金三冬先丙后壬」。不用于木／火／土／水日主的逐月取用（各转 bazi-qiongtong-mu／huo／tu／shui），不用于五行总纲（转 bazi-qiongtong-wu-xing），不排盘、不判流年大运、不断终身。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-qiongtong-jin))
- **bazi-qiongtong-mu**: 用于用户问《窮通寶鑑》「论木」里甲木、乙木逐月（正月到十二月，合三春三夏三秋三冬）怎么取用——木的性情与生木死木之别、「正月甲木得丙癸逢名寒木向阳」「二月甲木庚金得所名阳刃驾杀」「三春乙木丙癸不可离」「甲木用庚还是用丁」这类句子怎么讲。触发信号包括「论木」「甲木正月取用」「甲木用庚用丁」「乙木三春丙癸」「木性腾上而无所止」「生木得火而秀死木得金而造」「穷通宝鉴甲木」。不用于五行总纲（转 bazi-qiongtong-wu-xing）、不用于丙丁火（转 bazi-qiongtong-huo），不排盘、不批流年大运、不断终身。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-qiongtong-mu))
- **bazi-qiongtong-shui**: 用于用户拿壬水或癸水日主、问某个月（正月…十二月、或三春／三夏／三秋／三冬）该取哪几个字、次序怎么排时。触发信号包括「壬水日主正月取什么」「癸水八月辛金为用丙火佐之怎么讲」「水冷金寒爱丙丁什么意思」「壬水七月专用戊土」「癸水三冬专用丙火解冻」。不用于木／火／土／金日主的逐月取用（各转 bazi-qiongtong-mu／huo／tu／jin），不用于五行总纲（转 bazi-qiongtong-wu-xing），不排盘、不判流年大运、不断终身。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-qiongtong-shui))
- **bazi-qiongtong-tu**: 用于用户拿戊土或己土日主、问某个月（正月…十二月、或三春／三夏／三秋／三冬）该取哪几个字、次序怎么排时。触发信号包括「戊土日主正月取什么」「己土三秋先癸后丙怎么讲」「三春戊土无丙照暖是什么意思」「土逢季月见金多怎么解」「戊土十月先用甲木还是先丙」。不用于木／火／金／水日主的逐月取用（各转 bazi-qiongtong-mu／huo／jin／shui），不用于五行总纲（转 bazi-qiongtong-wu-xing），不排盘、不判流年大运、不断终身。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-qiongtong-tu))
- **bazi-qiongtong-wu-xing**: 用于用户问《窮通寶鑑》开篇「五行总论」怎么读——五行从何而来、为什么叫「行」、水木火金土各自的性向、相生相克为何合称「有伦」、为什么「五行皆赖土」、形色与数目怎么看，以及「过与不及，皆为乖道」「贵在折衷，归於中道」在全书的地位。触发信号包括「五行总论」「五行为什么叫行」「五行者本乎天地之间而不穷者也」「火为太阳水为太阴」「五行皆赖土」「水一火二木三金四土五」「过与不及皆为乖道」。不用于某一日主逐月取用（转 bazi-qiongtong-mu/-huo/-tu/-jin/-shui），不排盘、不批流年大运、不断终身。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-qiongtong-wu-xing))
- **bazi-reading**: Interpret a completed single-person BaZi JSON chart or equivalent verified four-pillar data as an evidence-linked static natal report. Use when a calculator hands off its artifact or someone asks to 解读八字, 看命局, explain day-master strength, structure, or favorable elements from an existing chart. Not for raw birth details, relationship matching, luck cycles, dated predictions, or incomplete source data. - **bazi-relationship**: Use when用户询问八字中的恋爱、伴侣、婚姻、夫妻互动、关系冲突，或这些主题的具体年份与验证窗口。 ([AITCX08/xuanji-bazi-skills](https://github.com/AITCX08/xuanji-bazi-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-relationship))
- **bazi-report**: 八字报告下单与进度查询技能 / Skill for bazi report checkout and progress tracking. 触发于“我要/我想买 2026 报告”“我想看人生/感情/财运/健康深度报告”“查报告进度/在哪里下载”等请求 / Trigger when users ask to buy 2026 report, buy deep reports (life/love/wealth/career), check progress, or ask where to download; then place order, check status, and return download page when ready. - **bazi-residence**: Use when用户询问八字中的搬家、租房、买房、装修、同居、分居、城市迁移、定居或相关年份。 ([AITCX08/xuanji-bazi-skills](https://github.com/AITCX08/xuanji-bazi-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-residence))
- **bazi-roundtable**: 命理圆桌会——多流派命理师（盲派、子平格局派、调候派、民国新派）对同一八字命盘进行圆桌论命：多视角总评、交叉质疑、可证伪断语、逐轮记分复盘。用户提到八字、四柱、命盘分析、批命、论流年大运、盲派/格局/调候论命、"多个命理师讨论"、验证式论命时使用本 skill；即使用户只是贴出一张八字排盘求分析，也应启用（可用轻量模式）。Multi-school Chinese BaZi (Four Pillars) roundtable reading: several fortune-tellers from rival schools analyze the same natal chart, cross-examine each other, issue falsifiable predictions, and keep score. Use whenever the user asks about BaZi, Four Pillars, Chinese astrology chart reading, or destiny analysis. ([yanouyuan-bit/bazi-roundtable](https://github.com/yanouyuan-bit/bazi-roundtable/tree/HEAD/bazi-roundtable) · ★32)
- **bazi-sanming-cai**: 用于用户问《三命通會》里正财与偏财各怎么立名、各主什么样的人、财多身弱与财宜藏露怎么讲、偏正财合论有哪几类财时。触发信号包括「正財者甲見己乙見戊」「偏財乃甲見戊乙見己」「財多身弱」「財宜藏露則浮蕩」「偏正二財喜忌大同」「財旺生官」「財為養命之源」「生成財」「財庫」。不用于问格局總論与立格条件（转 bazi-sanming-ge-ju）、不问官煞去留（转 bazi-sanming-guan-sha）、不问印綬（转 bazi-sanming-yin-shou）、不问食神伤官分论（转 bazi-sanming-shi-shang）、不批流年大运、不断终身吉凶。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-sanming-cai))
- **bazi-sanming-di-zhi**: 用于用户问《三命通會》里十二地支各自的体象、所藏、以及「十二支分配地理」的取象时。触发信号包括「子十二支之魁怎么讲」「丑中己土辛金究竟藏什么」「寅建于春气聚三阳什么意思」「辰为水库为什么又叫草泽」「未中有乙木丁火是藏官藏印」「戌乃洪鑪之库」「十二支取名之义子丑寅卯各从何来」「地支到底藏不藏干」「大抵五行用法总无真实生死衰旺亦假名耳」。不用于十天干体象（转 bazi-sanming-tian-gan）、不问月令人元司令几日的细目（转 bazi-sanming-ren-yuan）、不问纳音（转 bazi-sanming-na-yin）、不排盘、不批流年大运、不断终身、不判寿夭疾病。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-sanming-di-zhi))
- **bazi-sanming-ge-ju**: 用于用户问《三命通會》里「格局」是怎么立起来的、子平为什么只以印食官財四者为纲、六格从哪儿推出来、正官偏官杂气这些格各凭什么条件成立时。触发信号包括「子平为什么只论财官印食」「格局是从哪推出来的」「正官格怎么算」「杂气财官是什么」「入格者非富即贵怎么讲」；给了四柱可照书落盘读一遍。不用于问官煞去留与官鬼互变（转 bazi-sanming-guan-sha）、不问财星偏正分论（转 bazi-sanming-cai）、不问印綬与倒食（转 bazi-sanming-yin-shou）、不问食神伤官（转 bazi-sanming-shi-shang）、不问喜忌篇的格名断语（转 bazi-sanming-xi-ji-pian）、不批流年大运、不断终身吉凶。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-sanming-ge-ju))
- **bazi-sanming-guan-sha**: 用于用户问《三命通會》里正官与七煞同时出现该怎么「去留」、官化为鬼或鬼化为官是怎么变时。触发信号包括「官煞去留」「去官留煞还是去煞留官」「官煞混雜怎么论」「鬼化官官化鬼」「官煞兩停喜者存之憎者去之」「合煞去官留煞」「旺迅逄鬼鬼化官」「煞官混雜不貧則夭」；给了四柱可照书落盘读一遍。不用于问格局總論与立格条件（转 bazi-sanming-ge-ju）、不问财星分论（转 bazi-sanming-cai）、不问印綬（转 bazi-sanming-yin-shou）、不问食神伤官分论（转 bazi-sanming-shi-shang）、不问六神篇的十神互制（转 bazi-sanming-liu-shen）、不批流年大运、不断终身吉凶。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-sanming-guan-sha))
- **bazi-sanming-ji-shan-pian**: 用于用户问《三命通會》「繼善篇」里以日干为主、月令为提纲、用神与日主该怎样看的道理时。触发信号包括「繼善篇」「欲知貴賤先觀月令乃提綱」「次斷吉凶專用日干為主本」「用神不可損傷」「日主最宜健旺」「取用憑於生月」「發覺在於日時」「財多身弱正為富屋貧人」「非夭即貧必是身衰遇鬼」「身强煞淺假煞為權」「衰則變官為鬼」「庚辛來傷甲乙丙丁先見無危」「五行不可偏枯務稟中和」。不用于问十神间如何牵制翻覆（转 bazi-sanming-liu-shen）、不问喜忌篇逐条的格名宜忌（转 bazi-sanming-xi-ji-pian）、不问气象规模（转 bazi-sanming-qi-xiang-pian）、不批流年大运、不断终身。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-sanming-ji-shan-pian))
- **bazi-sanming-liu-shen**: 用于用户问《三命通會》「六神篇」里官、煞、印、財、食、傷这几样十神之间怎么互相牵制与翻覆时。触发信号包括「六神篇」「正官佩印不如乘馬」「七煞用財豈宜得祿」「印逢財而罷職」「食神孑立逢梟」「羊刃重逢破局」「貪財壞印」「一仁可化」「一馬在廐」「子位子填」；给了四柱可照书落盘读一遍。不用于问某个八字成不成某个格局（转 bazi-sanming-ge-ju）、不问喜忌篇列出的格名断语（转 bazi-sanming-xi-ji-pian）、不问以日干月令为纲的取用次序（转 bazi-sanming-ji-shan-pian）、不问气象规模的清浊刚柔（转 bazi-sanming-qi-xiang-pian）、不批流年大运、不断终身吉凶。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-sanming-liu-shen))

- **bazi-sanming-na-yin**: 用于用户问《三命通會》里的纳音——六十甲子纳音怎么定、纳音到底是什么、海中金炉中火这些名目怎么取象、同類娶妻隔八生子是什么意思、纳音还值不值得用、「甲子乙丑海中金」为什么这么配时。触发信号包括「六十甲子纳音怎么来的」「纳音同類娶妻隔八生子怎么讲」「甲子乙丑为什么是海中金」「纳音取象出自黄帝对不对」「纳音和正五行什么关系」「纳音为身是什么意思」。不用于十天干体象（转 bazi-sanming-tian-gan）、不问十二支体象与藏遁（转 bazi-sanming-di-zhi）、不问人元司令（转 bazi-sanming-ren-yuan）、不排盘、不批流年大运、不断终身、不判寿夭疾病。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-sanming-na-yin))
- **bazi-sanming-nu-ming**: 用于用户读《三命通會》卷七「論女命」、问女命凭什么星为夫为子、八法八格（純·和·清·貴·濁·濫·娼·淫）怎么分、月支财官印三格怎么取、夫星怎么定。触发信号包括「女命以克我者为夫是什么意思」「先观夫星再看子星怎么讲」「女命以柔为本以刚为刑」「纯者一也清者洁净之称」「论女命只用月支财官印三件为奇」。不用于日時斷（转 bazi-sanming-ri-shi-yang／bazi-sanming-ri-shi-yin）、不批流年大运、不断终身；严禁依据八字给疾病诊断、寿命预测、生育判断、婚姻决定；书里「克夫／贱／淫」这类词只作书里的说法，不落到具体人身上。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-sanming-nu-ming))
- **bazi-sanming-qi-xiang-pian**: 用于用户问《三命通會》「氣象篇」（醉醒子撰）里「先觀氣象規模乃富貴貧賤之綱領」「次論用神出處盡死生窮達之精微」这套看整体气象的道理时。触发信号包括「氣象篇」「先觀氣象規模乃富貴貧賤之綱領」「陽剛不中亢則害也」「柔弱偏枯小人之象剛健中正君子之風」「過於寒薄和煖處終難奮發」「五行各得其所者歸聚成福」「一局皆失其垣者流蕩無依」「一將當關羣邪自服」「六合有功權尊六部」「三刑得用威鎮三邊」。不用于问十神间如何牵制翻覆（转 bazi-sanming-liu-shen）、不问喜忌篇逐条格名（转 bazi-sanming-xi-ji-pian）、不问以月令日干为纲的取用次序（转 bazi-sanming-ji-shan-pian）、不批流年大运、不断终身。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-sanming-qi-xiang-pian))
- **bazi-sanming-ren-yuan**: 用于用户问《三命通會》里「人元司事」与月令分野——支中所藏为什么叫司事之神、月令里哪个干当令几日、玉井与醉醒子两套分法各是什么、为什么说「支中所藏止以月论」时。触发信号包括「人元司令是什么」「正月建寅艮土用事五日丙火长生五日甲木二十日怎么讲」「月令分野怎么分」「为什么用神取月令」「玉井甲丙庚壬各三十五日是什么意思」「土旺四季各十八日」「为什么说支中所藏止以月论」。不用于十天干体象（转 bazi-sanming-tian-gan）、不问十二支体象与取象（转 bazi-sanming-di-zhi）、不问纳音（转 bazi-sanming-na-yin）、不排盘、不批流年大运、不断终身、不判寿夭疾病。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-sanming-ren-yuan))
- **bazi-sanming-ri-shi-yang**: 用于用户读《三命通會》「日時斷」、问阳干日主（六甲六丙六戊六庚六壬）在十二时上怎么取断、时上歌诀与所忌月分怎么读时。触发信号包括「日时断怎么看」「六甲日生甲子时是什么意思」「甲丙戊庚壬日时上怎么论」「所忌月分与时同断怎么讲」「日时为主年月为辅是什么意思」。不用于阴干日主（转 bazi-sanming-ri-shi-yin）、不接女命与夫星（转 bazi-sanming-nu-ming）、不批流年大运、不断终身、不把书里的断语落到具体人身上。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-sanming-ri-shi-yang))

- **bazi-ziping-gan-zhi-xing-qing**: 用于用户问《子平真詮》前十干十二支与性情这一路怎么读时——例如「天地之间，一气而已」怎么分阴阳四象、为什么五行之外又有十干十二支、同是克木为什么分出官与煞、阴阳生死为什么阳顺阴逆、十干配合的性情与合而不合怎么分别、得时不旺失时不弱为什么是「活看」、刑冲怎么用会合来解。也用于把这套口径落到某一组四柱上逐条核（合化向背、有根无根、刑冲被会合解否）。不用于月令用神与格局成败（转 bazi-ziping-yong-shen-zong-gang 与本族用神诸篇）、不用于行运（转 bazi-ziping-xing-yun）、不批流年大运、不断终身寿夭、不判疾病生育、不替用户做婚姻或职业决定。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-ziping-gan-zhi-xing-qing))
- **bazi-ziping-gong-fen-liu-qin**: 用于用户问《子平真詮》怎么「配六亲」时——六亲按年月日时宫位自上而下怎么排（宫分法）、又按十神怎么定（正印为母、偏财为父、正财为妻、官煞为子女、比肩为兄弟），以及「论妻子」里妻宫妻星怎么看、子息先看时支怎么讲。触发信号包括「六亲怎么配」「正印为母偏财为父」「正财为妻」「官煞为什么是子女」「比肩为兄弟」「妻宫妻星」「坐下财官妻当贤贵」「看子息先看时支」。不用于问用神成败变化（转 bazi-ziping-yong-shen-zong-gang）、不问相神杂气（转 bazi-ziping-xiang-shen-za-qi）、不留断克夫克子、不断婚姻去留、不判生育与寿夭、不批流年大运。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-ziping-gong-fen-liu-qin))
- **bazi-ziping-ji-xiong-shen**: 用于用户问《子平真詮》里四吉神（财官印食）为什么也能破格、四凶神（煞伤枭刃）为什么也能成格、同一组十神生克为什么因先后不同而吉凶两样、星辰神煞与格局成败到底有没有关系、外格什么时候可以用什么时候该舍时。触发信号包括「財官印食不是吉神吗怎么会破格」「煞傷梟刃不是凶神吗怎么能成格」「同是財傷並透为什么先丁后戊和先戊后丁不一样」「神煞貴人孤辰到底算不算数」「外格什么时候用什么时候舍」。不用于问用神总纲与成败救应（转 bazi-ziping-yong-shen-zong-gang）、不问相神与杂气取用（转 bazi-ziping-xiang-shen-za-qi）、不问行运成格变格（转 bazi-ziping-xing-yun）、不批流年大运、不断终身吉凶。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/bazi-ziping-ji-xiong-shen))
